- **spec-md**: The single /spec-md skill for *.spec.md work. It authors and maintains OKF specs that keep intent, behavior, and verification aligned across humans and agents, and it checks specs or reports test-case coverage. The skill identifies which job the request needs, or presents the options when the intent is unclear. One root SKILL.md — no commands and no nested skills — so it surfaces as the bare /spec-md, with no colon-suffixed sub-commands. ([rosenjcb/spec-md](https://github.com/rosenjcb/spec-md) · ★1 · 2 skills)
- **spec-mirror**: Six-skill safety net: generate, drift-check, lint, test-stub, coverage-audit, and scope-walk E2E specs against the current implementation ([edonghyun/donghyuns-agent-tools](https://github.com/edonghyun/donghyuns-agent-tools/tree/HEAD/plugins/spec-mirror) · ★1 · 6 skills)
- **spec-ops**: A spec-driven development workflow that turns a rough idea into a verified implementation. It elicits and hardens an acceptance-criteria-first spec against your codebase, compiles it into an execution driver, then checks the built code against every criterion — with read-only adversarial judges gating quality at each stage and every spec change committed automatically. When OpenAI Codex CLI is available it adds an optional, fail-open second judge of a different provider, so every quality gate is cross-checked rather than one model auditing itself. ([zakattack9/agentic-coding](https://github.com/zakattack9/agentic-coding/tree/HEAD/claude-code/plugins/spec-ops) · ★1 · 6 skills, 3 agents)
- **spec-oracle-review**: Blind, spec-derived PR review. Write the test plan from the SPEC/ADR/issue before reading the implementation, then diff plan against code and tests — with probe tests and single-line mutations — to separate spec ambiguity, implementation defects, harness gaps, and unreachable requirements. Run it before diff-anchored cross-agent rounds. ([enkira-ai/claude-plugins](https://github.com/enkira-ai/claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/spec-oracle-review) · ★2 · 1 skill, 1 command)
- **spec-pipeline**: Spec-driven autonomous development pipeline: author a master spec, decompose into phases, execute each phase under TDD with deterministic specpipe validator gates (structure, dependency graphs, decision-id citations, TDD step order, RED/GREEN evidence, round caps) in front of every review pass.
